					weight = 1
					reference = 1000
					start_weight = -0.4
				}
				air_agility = {
					reference = 200
					weight = 0.4	
					start_weight = -0.4
				}
				anti_air = {
					reference = 6
					weight = -0.5
				}
				interception = {
					reference = 12 
					weight = -0.15
				}
				intel = {
					weight = 0.1
					reference = 100
				}
			}

			disaster = {
				base = 0.05
				anti_air = {
					reference = 6
					weight = 0.30
				}
				interception = {
					reference = 12 
					weight = 0.15
				}
				air_agility = {
					reference = 200
					weight = -0.30
				}
			}

			critical = {
				base = 0.01
				air_agility = {
					reference = 200
					start_reference = 100
					weight = 0.05
				}
				strategic_bomber = {
					weight = 0.50
					reference = 3000
					start_reference = 1100
				}
			}
		}
		success_levels = {
			failure = {
				victim_effects = {
					set_temp_variable = {
						missle_strike_dmg = 0
					}
					custom_effect_tooltip = {
						localization_key = bridges_raid_losses_tt
						LOSSES = [?missle_strike_dmg]
					}
				}
				actor_effects = {
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 20 }

						localization_key = plane_raid_losses_tt

					}

							ratio = no
						}
					}

					}

					custom_effect_tooltip = {

					}
				}
				actor_effects = {
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lostt = 20 }
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 1 }

					var:target_state = {
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.aa_defense = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@anti_air_building }
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.radar_mult = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@radar_station }
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.rocket_mult = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@rocket_site }
					}

					add_to_temp_variable = { radar_mult = 0.5 }
					multiply_temp_variable = { aa_defense = radar_mult }
					multiply_temp_variable = { rocket_mult = radar_mult }

						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.aa_defense = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@anti_air_building }
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.radar_mult = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@radar_station }
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.rocket_mult = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@rocket_site }
					}

					add_to_temp_variable = { radar_mult = 0.5 }
					multiply_temp_variable = { aa_defense = radar_mult }
					multiply_temp_variable = { rocket_mult = radar_mult }

					if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = { check_variable = { rocket_mult = 0 } }
						}
						multiply_temp_variable = { aa_defense = rocket_mult }

						if = {
							limit = {
								check_variable = { aa_defense = 0 }
							}
							add_to_temp_variable = { aa_defense = rocket_mult }
						}
					}

					multiply_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = aa_defense integer = yes }

					clamp_temp_variable = {
						var = raid_planes_lost
						min = 0
					}

					round_temp_variable = raid_planes_lost

					if = { 
						limit = { 
							check_variable = { raid_planes_lost > 2 } 
						} 
						set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 2 } 
					}

					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = raid_planes_lostt }	# missiles actually expended (shown by the loc)
					custom_effect_tooltip = {
						localization_key = missiles_raid_losses_tt
					}

					hidden_effect = {
						raid_damage_units = {
							plane_loss = raid_planes_lostt
							ratio = no
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

					start_reference = 100
				}
			}

		success_levels = {

				victim_effects = {
					set_temp_variable = {
						missle_strike_dmg = 0
					}
					custom_effect_tooltip = {

						LOSSES = [?missle_strike_dmg]
					}
				}
				actor_effects = {

				actor_effects = {
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lostt = 100 }
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 1 }

					var:target_state = {
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.aa_defense = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@anti_air_building }
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.radar_mult = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@radar_station }
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.rocket_mult = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@rocket_site }
					}

					add_to_temp_variable = { radar_mult = 0.5 }
					multiply_temp_variable = { aa_defense = radar_mult }
					multiply_temp_variable = { rocket_mult = radar_mult }

							NOT = { check_variable = { rocket_mult = 0 } }

	strikes_on_bridges_CHERKASI_STRAIT_bl = {
		days_to_prepare = 1
		days_re_enable = 3
		category = on_bridge_strikes
		command_power = 0
		 ai_will_do = {
		 	base = 1.0
		 	modifier = {
		 		factor = 5.0
		 		date > 2022.3.1
		 	}
		 	modifier = {
		 		factor = 0.2
		 		date < 2022.3.1
		 	}
		 }
		allowed = {
			has_dlc = "Gotterdammerung"
		}
		visible = {
		}
		show_target = {
			has_war_with = FROM
			check_variable = { global.dnipro_obj_current_hp^9 > 0 }	# bridge not destroyed
		}
		available = {
			has_war_with = FROM
			check_variable = { global.dnipro_obj_current_hp^9 > 0 }	# bridge not destroyed
		}
		launchable = {
			check_variable = { global.dnipro_obj_current_hp^9 > 0 }	# bridge not destroyed
		}
		target_type = {
			province = 6558
		}
		target_icon = GFX_other_target_icon
		launch_sound = raid_launch_air
		arrow = {
			type = air
		}
		unit_model = {
		}
		unit_requirements = {
			equipment = {
				type = { ballistic_missile_equipment rus_heavy_guided_missile_equipment ballistic_missile_chassis }
				amount = { min = 12 }
			}
		}
		starting_point = {
			types = {
				rocket_site
			}
		}
		success_factors = {
			success = {
				base = 0.1
				strategic_bomber = {
					weight = 1
					reference = 1000
					start_weight = -0.4
				}
				air_agility = {
					reference = 200
					weight = 0.4	
					start_weight = -0.4
				}
				anti_air = {
					reference = 6
					weight = -0.5
				}
				intel = {
					weight = 0.1
					reference = 100
				}
			}

			disaster = {
				base = 0.05
				anti_air = {
					reference = 6
					weight = 0.30
				}
				air_agility = {
					reference = 200
					weight = -0.30
				}
			}

			critical = {
				base = 0.01
				air_agility = {
					reference = 200
					start_reference = 100
					weight = 0.05
				}
				strategic_bomber = {
					weight = 0.50
					reference = 3000
					start_reference = 1100
				}
			}
		}
		success_levels = {
			failure = {
				victim_effects = {
					set_temp_variable = {
						missle_strike_dmg = 0
					}
					custom_effect_tooltip = {
						localization_key = bridges_raid_losses_tt
						LOSSES = [?missle_strike_dmg]
					}
				}
				actor_effects = {
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 12 }
					
					custom_effect_tooltip = {
						localization_key = plane_raid_losses_tt
						LOSSES = [?raid_planes_lost]
					}

					hidden_effect = {
						raid_damage_units = {
							plane_loss = raid_planes_lost
							ratio = no
						}
					}
				}
			}
			limited_success = {
				victim_effects = {
					set_temp_variable = {
						missle_strike_dmg = 40
					}
					subtract_from_variable = {
						global.dnipro_obj_current_hp^9 = missle_strike_dmg
					}
					custom_effect_tooltip = {
						localization_key = bridges_raid_losses_tt
						LOSSES = [?missle_strike_dmg]
					}
				}
				actor_effects = {
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lostt = 12 }
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 1 }

					var:target_state = {
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.aa_defense = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@anti_air_building }
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.radar_mult = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@radar_station }
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.rocket_mult = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@rocket_site }
					}

					add_to_temp_variable = { radar_mult = 0.5 }
					multiply_temp_variable = { aa_defense = radar_mult }
					multiply_temp_variable = { rocket_mult = radar_mult }

					if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = { check_variable = { rocket_mult = 0 } }
						}
						multiply_temp_variable = { aa_defense = rocket_mult }

						if = {
							limit = {
								check_variable = { aa_defense = 0 }
							}
							add_to_temp_variable = { aa_defense = rocket_mult }
						}
					}

					multiply_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = aa_defense integer = yes }

					clamp_temp_variable = {
						var = raid_planes_lost
						min = 0
					}

					round_temp_variable = raid_planes_lost

					if = { 
						limit = { 
							check_variable = { raid_planes_lost > 3 } 
						} 
						set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 3 } 
					}

					if = { 
						limit = { 
							check_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 0 } 
						} 
						set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 2 } 
					}
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = raid_planes_lostt }	# missiles actually expended (shown by the loc)
					custom_effect_tooltip = {
						localization_key = missiles_raid_losses_tt
					}

					hidden_effect = {
						raid_damage_units = {
							plane_loss = raid_planes_lostt
							ratio = no
						}
					}
				}
				
			}
			success = {
				victim_effects = {
					# Random damage is applied hidden and stored; the result popup re-evaluates this block
					# after execution with a new roll, so the tooltip must read the stored value.
					hidden_effect = {
						set_temp_variable_to_random = {
							var = missle_strike_dmg
							min = 40
							max = 80
							integer = yes
						}
						subtract_from_variable = {
							global.dnipro_obj_current_hp^9 = missle_strike_dmg
						}
						set_variable = { global.raid_last_bridge_dmg = missle_strike_dmg }
					}
					set_temp_variable = { missle_strike_dmg = global.raid_last_bridge_dmg }	# stored real damage (shown by the loc)
					custom_effect_tooltip = {
						localization_key = bridges_raid_losses_tt
					}
				}
				actor_effects = {
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lostt = 12 }
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 1 }

					var:target_state = {
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.aa_defense = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@anti_air_building }
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.radar_mult = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@radar_station }
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.rocket_mult = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@rocket_site }
					}

					add_to_temp_variable = { radar_mult = 0.5 }
					multiply_temp_variable = { aa_defense = radar_mult }
					multiply_temp_variable = { rocket_mult = radar_mult }

					if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = { check_variable = { rocket_mult = 0 } }
						}
						multiply_temp_variable = { aa_defense = rocket_mult }

						if = {
							limit = {
								check_variable = { aa_defense = 0 }
							}
							add_to_temp_variable = { aa_defense = rocket_mult }
						}
					}

					multiply_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = aa_defense integer = yes }

					clamp_temp_variable = {
						var = raid_planes_lost
						min = 0
					}

					round_temp_variable = raid_planes_lost

					if = { 
						limit = { 
							check_variable = { raid_planes_lost > 3 } 
						} 
						set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 3 } 
					}

					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = raid_planes_lostt }	# missiles actually expended (shown by the loc)
					custom_effect_tooltip = {
						localization_key = missiles_raid_losses_tt
					}

					hidden_effect = {
						raid_damage_units = {
							plane_loss = raid_planes_lostt
							ratio = no
						}
					}
				}
				
			}
			critical_success = {
				victim_effects = {
					set_temp_variable = {
						missle_strike_dmg = 100
					}
					subtract_from_variable = {
						global.dnipro_obj_current_hp^9 = missle_strike_dmg
					}
					custom_effect_tooltip = {
						localization_key = bridges_raid_losses_tt
						LOSSES = [?missle_strike_dmg]
					}
				}
				actor_effects = {
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lostt = 12 }
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 0 }

					var:target_state = {
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.aa_defense = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@anti_air_building }
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.radar_mult = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@radar_station }
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.rocket_mult = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@rocket_site }
					}

					add_to_temp_variable = { radar_mult = 0.5 }
					multiply_temp_variable = { aa_defense = radar_mult }
					multiply_temp_variable = { rocket_mult = radar_mult }

					if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = { check_variable = { rocket_mult = 0 } }
						}
						multiply_temp_variable = { aa_defense = rocket_mult }

						if = {
							limit = {
								check_variable = { aa_defense = 0 }
							}
							add_to_temp_variable = { aa_defense = rocket_mult }
						}
					}

					multiply_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = aa_defense integer = yes }

					clamp_temp_variable = {
						var = raid_planes_lost
						min = 0
					}

					round_temp_variable = raid_planes_lost

					if = { 
						limit = { 
							check_variable = { raid_planes_lost > 2 } 
						} 
						set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 2 } 
					}

					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = raid_planes_lostt }	# missiles actually expended (shown by the loc)
					custom_effect_tooltip = {
						localization_key = missiles_raid_losses_tt
					}

					hidden_effect = {
						raid_damage_units = {
							plane_loss = raid_planes_lostt
							ratio = no
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

				interception = {
					reference = 12 
					weight = -0.15
				}
				intel = {
					weight = 0.1
					reference = 100
				}
			}

			disaster = {
				base = 0.05
				anti_air = {
					reference = 6
					weight = 0.30
				}
				interception = {
					reference = 12 
					weight = 0.15
				}
				air_agility = {
					reference = 200
					weight = -0.30
				}
			}

			critical = {
				base = 0.01
				air_agility = {
					reference = 200
					start_reference = 100
					weight = 0.05
				}
				strategic_bomber = {
					weight = 0.50
					reference = 3000
					start_reference = 1100
				}
			}
		}
		success_levels = {
			failure = {
				victim_effects = {
					set_temp_variable = {
						missle_strike_dmg = 0
					}
					custom_effect_tooltip = {
						localization_key = bridges_raid_losses_tt
						LOSSES = [?missle_strike_dmg]
					}
				}
				actor_effects = {
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 20 }
					
					custom_effect_tooltip = {
						localization_key = plane_raid_losses_tt
						LOSSES = [?raid_planes_lost]
					}

					hidden_effect = {
						raid_damage_units = {
							plane_loss = raid_planes_lost
							ratio = no
						}
					}
				}
			}
			limited_success = {
				victim_effects = {
					set_temp_variable = {
						missle_strike_dmg = 20
					}
					subtract_from_variable = {
						global.dnipro_obj_current_hp^11 = missle_strike_dmg
					}
					custom_effect_tooltip = {
						localization_key = bridges_raid_losses_tt
						LOSSES = [?missle_strike_dmg]
					}
				}
				actor_effects = {
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lostt = 20 }
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 1 }

					var:target_state = {
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.aa_defense = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@anti_air_building }
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.radar_mult = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@radar_station }
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.rocket_mult = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@rocket_site }
					}

					add_to_temp_variable = { radar_mult = 0.5 }
					multiply_temp_variable = { aa_defense = radar_mult }
					multiply_temp_variable = { rocket_mult = radar_mult }

					if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = { check_variable = { rocket_mult = 0 } }
						}
						multiply_temp_variable = { aa_defense = rocket_mult }

						if = {
							limit = {
								check_variable = { aa_defense = 0 }
							}
							add_to_temp_variable = { aa_defense = rocket_mult }
						}
					}

					multiply_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = aa_defense integer = yes }

					clamp_temp_variable = {
						var = raid_planes_lost
						min = 0
					}

					round_temp_variable = raid_planes_lost

					if = { 
						limit = { 
							check_variable = { raid_planes_lost > 3 } 
						} 
						set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 3 } 
					}

					if = { 
						limit = { 
							check_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 0 } 
						} 
						set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 2 } 
					}
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = raid_planes_lostt }	# missiles actually expended (shown by the loc)
					custom_effect_tooltip = {
						localization_key = missiles_raid_losses_tt
					}

					hidden_effect = {
						raid_damage_units = {
							plane_loss = raid_planes_lostt
							ratio = no
						}
					}
				}
			}
			success = {
				victim_effects = {
					# Random damage is applied hidden and stored; the result popup re-evaluates this block
					# after execution with a new roll, so the tooltip must read the stored value.
					hidden_effect = {
						set_temp_variable_to_random = {
							var = missle_strike_dmg
							min = 20
							max = 40
							integer = yes
						}
						subtract_from_variable = {
							global.dnipro_obj_current_hp^11 = missle_strike_dmg
						}
						set_variable = { global.raid_last_bridge_dmg = missle_strike_dmg }
					}
					set_temp_variable = { missle_strike_dmg = global.raid_last_bridge_dmg }	# stored real damage (shown by the loc)
					custom_effect_tooltip = {
						localization_key = bridges_raid_losses_tt
					}
				}
				actor_effects = {
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lostt = 20 }
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 1 }

					var:target_state = {
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.aa_defense = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@anti_air_building }

					}

					add_to_temp_variable = { radar_mult = 0.5 }
					multiply_temp_variable = { aa_defense = radar_mult }
					multiply_temp_variable = { rocket_mult = radar_mult }

					if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = { check_variable = { rocket_mult = 0 } }
						}
						multiply_temp_variable = { aa_defense = rocket_mult }

						if = {
							limit = {
								check_variable = { aa_defense = 0 }
							}
							add_to_temp_variable = { aa_defense = rocket_mult }
						}
					}

					multiply_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = aa_defense integer = yes }

					clamp_temp_variable = {
						var = raid_planes_lost
						min = 0
					}

					round_temp_variable = raid_planes_lost

					if = { 
						limit = { 
							check_variable = { raid_planes_lost > 3 } 
						} 
						set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 3 } 
					}

					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = raid_planes_lostt }	# missiles actually expended (shown by the loc)

					}

					hidden_effect = {
						raid_damage_units = {
							plane_loss = raid_planes_lostt
							ratio = no
						}
					}
				}
			}
			critical_success = {
				victim_effects = {
					set_temp_variable = {
						missle_strike_dmg = 50
					}
					subtract_from_variable = {
						global.dnipro_obj_current_hp^11 = missle_strike_dmg
					}
					custom_effect_tooltip = {
						localization_key = bridges_raid_losses_tt
						LOSSES = [?missle_strike_dmg]
					}
				}
				actor_effects = {
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lostt = 20 }
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 0 }

					var:target_state = {
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.aa_defense = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@anti_air_building }

						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.aa_defense = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@anti_air_building }
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.radar_mult = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@radar_station }
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.rocket_mult = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@rocket_site }
					}

					add_to_temp_variable = { radar_mult = 0.5 }
					multiply_temp_variable = { aa_defense = radar_mult }
					multiply_temp_variable = { rocket_mult = radar_mult }

					if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = { check_variable = { rocket_mult = 0 } }
						}
						multiply_temp_variable = { aa_defense = rocket_mult }

						if = {
							limit = {
								check_variable = { aa_defense = 0 }
							}
							add_to_temp_variable = { aa_defense = rocket_mult }
						}
					}

					multiply_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = aa_defense integer = yes }

					clamp_temp_variable = {
						var = raid_planes_lost
						min = 0
					}

					round_temp_variable = raid_planes_lost

					if = { 
						limit = { 
							check_variable = { raid_planes_lost > 12 } 
						} 
						set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 10 } 
					}

					if = { 
						limit = { 
							check_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 0 } 
						} 
						set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 4 } 
					}
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = raid_planes_lostt }	# missiles actually expended (shown by the loc)
					custom_effect_tooltip = {
						localization_key = missiles_raid_losses_tt
					}

					hidden_effect = {
						raid_damage_units = {
							plane_loss = raid_planes_lostt
							ratio = no
						}
					}
				}
				
			}
			success = {
				victim_effects = {
					# Random damage is applied hidden and stored; the result popup re-evaluates this block
					# after execution with a new roll, so the tooltip must read the stored value.
					hidden_effect = {
						set_temp_variable_to_random = {
							var = missle_strike_dmg
							min = 20
							max = 40
							integer = yes
						}
						subtract_from_variable = {
							global.dnipro_obj_current_hp^11 = missle_strike_dmg
						}
						set_variable = { global.raid_last_bridge_dmg = missle_strike_dmg }
					}
					set_temp_variable = { missle_strike_dmg = global.raid_last_bridge_dmg }	# stored real damage (shown by the loc)
					custom_effect_tooltip = {
						localization_key = bridges_raid_losses_tt
					}
				}
				actor_effects = {
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lostt = 100 }
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 1 }

					var:target_state = {
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.aa_defense = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@anti_air_building }
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.radar_mult = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@radar_station }
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.rocket_mult = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@rocket_site }
					}

					add_to_temp_variable = { radar_mult = 0.5 }
					multiply_temp_variable = { aa_defense = radar_mult }
					multiply_temp_variable = { rocket_mult = radar_mult }

					if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = { check_variable = { rocket_mult = 0 } }
						}
						multiply_temp_variable = { aa_defense = rocket_mult }

						if = {
							limit = {
								check_variable = { aa_defense = 0 }
							}
							add_to_temp_variable = { aa_defense = rocket_mult }
						}
					}

					multiply_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = aa_defense integer = yes }

					clamp_temp_variable = {
						var = raid_planes_lost
						min = 0
					}

					round_temp_variable = raid_planes_lost

					if = { 
						limit = { 
							check_variable = { raid_planes_lost > 10 } 
						} 
						set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 8 } 
					}

					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = raid_planes_lostt }	# missiles actually expended (shown by the loc)
					custom_effect_tooltip = {
						localization_key = missiles_raid_losses_tt
					}

					hidden_effect = {
						raid_damage_units = {
							plane_loss = raid_planes_lostt
							ratio = no
						}
					}
				}
				
			}
			critical_success = {
				victim_effects = {
					set_temp_variable = {
						missle_strike_dmg = 50
					}
					subtract_from_variable = {
						global.dnipro_obj_current_hp^11 = missle_strike_dmg
					}
					custom_effect_tooltip = {
						localization_key = bridges_raid_losses_tt
						LOSSES = [?missle_strike_dmg]
					}
				}
				actor_effects = {
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lostt = 100 }
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 1 }

					var:target_state = {
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.aa_defense = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@anti_air_building }
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.radar_mult = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@radar_station }
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.rocket_mult = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@rocket_site }
					}

					add_to_temp_variable = { radar_mult = 0.5 }
					multiply_temp_variable = { aa_defense = radar_mult }
					multiply_temp_variable = { rocket_mult = radar_mult }

					if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = { check_variable = { rocket_mult = 0 } }
						}
						multiply_temp_variable = { aa_defense = rocket_mult }

						if = {
							limit = {
								check_variable = { aa_defense = 0 }
							}
							add_to_temp_variable = { aa_defense = rocket_mult }
						}
					}

					multiply_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = aa_defense integer = yes }

					clamp_temp_variable = {
						var = raid_planes_lost
						min = 0
					}

					round_temp_variable = raid_planes_lost

					if = { 
						limit = { 
							check_variable = { raid_planes_lost > 8 } 
						} 
						set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 7 } 
					}

					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = raid_planes_lostt }	# missiles actually expended (shown by the loc)
					custom_effect_tooltip = {
						localization_key = missiles_raid_losses_tt
					}

					hidden_effect = {
						raid_damage_units = {
							plane_loss = raid_planes_lostt
							ratio = no
						}
					}
				}
				
			}
		}

					# after execution with a new roll, so the tooltip must read the stored value.
					hidden_effect = {
						set_temp_variable_to_random = {
							var = missle_strike_dmg
							min = 40
							max = 80
							integer = yes
						}
						subtract_from_variable = {
							global.dnipro_obj_current_hp^13 = missle_strike_dmg
						}
						set_variable = { global.raid_last_bridge_dmg = missle_strike_dmg }
					}
					set_temp_variable = { missle_strike_dmg = global.raid_last_bridge_dmg }	# stored real damage (shown by the loc)
					custom_effect_tooltip = {
						localization_key = bridges_raid_losses_tt
					}
				}
				actor_effects = {
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lostt = 12 }
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 1 }

					var:target_state = {
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.aa_defense = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@anti_air_building }
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.radar_mult = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@radar_station }
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.rocket_mult = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@rocket_site }
					}

					add_to_temp_variable = { radar_mult = 0.5 }
					multiply_temp_variable = { aa_defense = radar_mult }
					multiply_temp_variable = { rocket_mult = radar_mult }

					if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = { check_variable = { rocket_mult = 0 } }
						}
						multiply_temp_variable = { aa_defense = rocket_mult }

						if = {
							limit = {
								check_variable = { aa_defense = 0 }
							}
							add_to_temp_variable = { aa_defense = rocket_mult }
						}
					}

					multiply_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = aa_defense integer = yes }

					clamp_temp_variable = {
						var = raid_planes_lost
						min = 0
					}

					round_temp_variable = raid_planes_lost

					if = { 
						limit = { 
							check_variable = { raid_planes_lost > 3 } 
						} 
						set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 3 } 
					}

					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = raid_planes_lostt }	# missiles actually expended (shown by the loc)
					custom_effect_tooltip = {
						localization_key = missiles_raid_losses_tt
					}

					hidden_effect = {
						raid_damage_units = {
							plane_loss = raid_planes_lostt
							ratio = no
						}
					}
				}
				
			}
			critical_success = {
				victim_effects = {
					set_temp_variable = {
						missle_strike_dmg = 100
					}
					subtract_from_variable = {
						global.dnipro_obj_current_hp^13 = missle_strike_dmg
					}
					custom_effect_tooltip = {
						localization_key = bridges_raid_losses_tt
						LOSSES = [?missle_strike_dmg]
					}
				}
				actor_effects = {
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lostt = 12 }
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 0 }

					var:target_state = {
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.aa_defense = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@anti_air_building }
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.radar_mult = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@radar_station }
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.rocket_mult = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@rocket_site }
					}

					add_to_temp_variable = { radar_mult = 0.5 }
					multiply_temp_variable = { aa_defense = radar_mult }
					multiply_temp_variable = { rocket_mult = radar_mult }

					if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = { check_variable = { rocket_mult = 0 } }
						}
						multiply_temp_variable = { aa_defense = rocket_mult }

						if = {
							limit = {
								check_variable = { aa_defense = 0 }
							}
							add_to_temp_variable = { aa_defense = rocket_mult }
						}
					}

					multiply_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = aa_defense integer = yes }

					clamp_temp_variable = {
						var = raid_planes_lost
						min = 0
					}

					round_temp_variable = raid_planes_lost

					if = { 
						limit = { 
							check_variable = { raid_planes_lost > 2 } 
						} 
						set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 2 } 
					}

					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = raid_planes_lostt }	# missiles actually expended (shown by the loc)
					custom_effect_tooltip = {
						localization_key = missiles_raid_losses_tt
					}

					hidden_effect = {
						raid_damage_units = {
							plane_loss = raid_planes_lostt
							ratio = no
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}

					multiply_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = aa_defense integer = yes }

					clamp_temp_variable = {
						var = raid_planes_lost
						min = 0
					}

					round_temp_variable = raid_planes_lost

					if = { 
						limit = { 
							check_variable = { raid_planes_lost > 3 } 
						} 
						set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 3 } 
					}

					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = raid_planes_lostt }	# missiles actually expended (shown by the loc)
					custom_effect_tooltip = {
						localization_key = missiles_raid_losses_tt
					}

					hidden_effect = {
						raid_damage_units = {
							plane_loss = raid_planes_lostt
							ratio = no
						}
					}
				}
			}
			critical_success = {
				victim_effects = {
					set_temp_variable = {
						missle_strike_dmg = 50
					}
					subtract_from_variable = {
						global.dnipro_obj_current_hp^14 = missle_strike_dmg
					}
					custom_effect_tooltip = {
						localization_key = bridges_raid_losses_tt
						LOSSES = [?missle_strike_dmg]
					}
				}
				actor_effects = {
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lostt = 20 }
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 0 }

					var:target_state = {
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.aa_defense = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@anti_air_building }
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.radar_mult = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@radar_station }
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.rocket_mult = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@rocket_site }
					}

					add_to_temp_variable = { radar_mult = 0.5 }
					multiply_temp_variable = { aa_defense = radar_mult }
					multiply_temp_variable = { rocket_mult = radar_mult }

					if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = { check_variable = { rocket_mult = 0 } }
						}
						multiply_temp_variable = { aa_defense = rocket_mult }

						if = {
							limit = {
								check_variable = { aa_defense = 0 }
							}
							add_to_temp_variable = { aa_defense = rocket_mult }
						}
					}

					}

					multiply_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = aa_defense integer = yes }

					clamp_temp_variable = {
						var = raid_planes_lost
						min = 0
					}

					round_temp_variable = raid_planes_lost

					if = { 
						limit = { 
							check_variable = { raid_planes_lost > 8 } 
						} 
						set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 7 } 
					}

					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = raid_planes_lostt }	# missiles actually expended (shown by the loc)
					custom_effect_tooltip = {
						localization_key = missiles_raid_losses_tt
					}

					hidden_effect = {
						raid_damage_units = {
							plane_loss = raid_planes_lostt
							ratio = no
						}
					}
				}
				
			}
		}

	strikes_on_bridges_DNIPRO_AMUR_STRAIT_rc = {
		days_to_prepare = 1
		days_re_enable = 3
		category = on_bridge_strikes
		command_power = 0
		 ai_will_do = {
		 	base = 1.0
		 	modifier = {
		 		factor = 5.0
		 		date > 2022.3.1
		 	}
		 	modifier = {
		 		factor = 0.2
		 		date < 2022.3.1
		 	}
		 }
		allowed = {
			has_dlc = "Gotterdammerung"
		}
		visible = {
		}
		show_target = {
			has_war_with = FROM
			check_variable = { global.dnipro_obj_current_hp^15 > 0 }	# bridge not destroyed
		}
		available = {
			has_war_with = FROM
			check_variable = { global.dnipro_obj_current_hp^15 > 0 }	# bridge not destroyed
		}
		launchable = {
			check_variable = { global.dnipro_obj_current_hp^15 > 0 }	# bridge not destroyed
		}
		target_type = {
			province = 18751
		}
		target_icon = GFX_other_target_icon
		launch_sound = raid_launch_air
		arrow = {
			type = air
		}
		unit_model = {
		}
		unit_requirements = {
			equipment = {
				type = { rus_sea_guided_missile_equipment ukr_light_guided_missile_equipment ukr_sea_guided_missile_equipment nto_light_guided_missile_equipment nto_medium_guided_missile_equipment nto_sea_guided_missile_equipment rus_light_guided_missile_equipment rus_medium_guided_missile_equipment }
				amount = { min = 20 }
			}
		}
		starting_point = {
			types = {
				rocket_site
			}
		}
		success_factors = {
			success = {
				base = 0.1
				strategic_bomber = {
					weight = 1
					reference = 1000
					start_weight = -0.4
				}
				air_agility = {
					reference = 200
					weight = 0.4	
					start_weight = -0.4
				}
				anti_air = {
					reference = 6
					weight = -0.5
				}
				interception = {
					reference = 12 
					weight = -0.15
				}
				intel = {
					weight = 0.1
					reference = 100
				}
			}

			disaster = {
				base = 0.05
				anti_air = {
					reference = 6
					weight = 0.30
				}
				interception = {
					reference = 12 
					weight = 0.15
				}
				air_agility = {
					reference = 200
					weight = -0.30
				}
			}

			critical = {
				base = 0.01
				air_agility = {
					reference = 200
					start_reference = 100
					weight = 0.05
				}
				strategic_bomber = {
					weight = 0.50
					reference = 3000
					start_reference = 1100
				}
			}
		}
		success_levels = {
			failure = {
				victim_effects = {
					set_temp_variable = {
						missle_strike_dmg = 0
					}
					custom_effect_tooltip = {
						localization_key = bridges_raid_losses_tt
						LOSSES = [?missle_strike_dmg]
					}
				}
				actor_effects = {
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 20 }
					
					custom_effect_tooltip = {
						localization_key = plane_raid_losses_tt
						LOSSES = [?raid_planes_lost]
					}

					hidden_effect = {
						raid_damage_units = {
							plane_loss = raid_planes_lost
							ratio = no
						}
					}
				}
			}
			limited_success = {
				victim_effects = {
					set_temp_variable = {
						missle_strike_dmg = 20
					}
					subtract_from_variable = {
						global.dnipro_obj_current_hp^15 = missle_strike_dmg
					}
					custom_effect_tooltip = {
						localization_key = bridges_raid_losses_tt
						LOSSES = [?missle_strike_dmg]
					}
				}
				actor_effects = {
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lostt = 20 }
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 1 }

					var:target_state = {
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.aa_defense = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@anti_air_building }
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.radar_mult = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@radar_station }
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.rocket_mult = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@rocket_site }
					}

					add_to_temp_variable = { radar_mult = 0.5 }
					multiply_temp_variable = { aa_defense = radar_mult }
					multiply_temp_variable = { rocket_mult = radar_mult }

					if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = { check_variable = { rocket_mult = 0 } }
						}
						multiply_temp_variable = { aa_defense = rocket_mult }

						if = {
							limit = {
								check_variable = { aa_defense = 0 }
							}
							add_to_temp_variable = { aa_defense = rocket_mult }
						}
					}

					multiply_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = aa_defense integer = yes }

					clamp_temp_variable = {
						var = raid_planes_lost
						min = 0
					}

					round_temp_variable = raid_planes_lost

					if = { 
						limit = { 
							check_variable = { raid_planes_lost > 3 } 
						} 
						set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 3 } 
					}

					if = { 
						limit = { 
							check_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 0 } 
						} 
						set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 2 } 
					}
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = raid_planes_lostt }	# missiles actually expended (shown by the loc)
					custom_effect_tooltip = {
						localization_key = missiles_raid_losses_tt
					}

					hidden_effect = {
						raid_damage_units = {
							plane_loss = raid_planes_lostt
							ratio = no
						}
					}
				}
			}
			success = {
				victim_effects = {
					# Random damage is applied hidden and stored; the result popup re-evaluates this block
					# after execution with a new roll, so the tooltip must read the stored value.
					hidden_effect = {
						set_temp_variable_to_random = {
							var = missle_strike_dmg
							min = 20
							max = 40
							integer = yes
						}
						subtract_from_variable = {
							global.dnipro_obj_current_hp^15 = missle_strike_dmg
						}
						set_variable = { global.raid_last_bridge_dmg = missle_strike_dmg }
					}
					set_temp_variable = { missle_strike_dmg = global.raid_last_bridge_dmg }	# stored real damage (shown by the loc)
					custom_effect_tooltip = {
						localization_key = bridges_raid_losses_tt
					}
				}
				actor_effects = {
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lostt = 20 }
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 1 }

					var:target_state = {
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.aa_defense = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@anti_air_building }
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.radar_mult = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@radar_station }
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.rocket_mult = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@rocket_site }
					}

					add_to_temp_variable = { radar_mult = 0.5 }
					multiply_temp_variable = { aa_defense = radar_mult }
					multiply_temp_variable = { rocket_mult = radar_mult }

					if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = { check_variable = { rocket_mult = 0 } }
						}
						multiply_temp_variable = { aa_defense = rocket_mult }

						if = {
							limit = {
								check_variable = { aa_defense = 0 }
							}
							add_to_temp_variable = { aa_defense = rocket_mult }
						}
					}

					multiply_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = aa_defense integer = yes }

					clamp_temp_variable = {
						var = raid_planes_lost
						min = 0
					}

					round_temp_variable = raid_planes_lost

					if = { 
						limit = { 
							check_variable = { raid_planes_lost > 3 } 
						} 
						set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 3 }

					}

						raid_damage_units = {
							plane_loss = raid_planes_lostt
							ratio = no
						}
					}
				}
			}
			critical_success = {
				victim_effects = {
					set_temp_variable = {
						missle_strike_dmg = 50
					}
					subtract_from_variable = {
						global.dnipro_obj_current_hp^15 = missle_strike_dmg
					}
					custom_effect_tooltip = {
						localization_key = bridges_raid_losses_tt

				actor_effects = {

					var:target_state = {

		target_icon = GFX_other_target_icon
		launch_sound = raid_launch_air
		arrow = {
			type = air
		}
		unit_model = {
		}
		unit_requirements = {
			equipment = {
				type = { ukr_drone_missile_equipment nto_guided_uav_equipment ukr_guided_uav_equipment rus_guided_uav_equipment }
				amount = { min = 100 }
			}
		}
		starting_point = {
			types = {
				rocket_site
			}
		}
		success_factors = {
			success = {
				base = 0.1
				strategic_bomber = {
					weight = 0.6
					reference = 100
					start_weight = -0.10
				}
				air_agility = {
					reference = 20
					weight = 0.4
					start_weight = -0.10
				}
				anti_air = {
					reference = 6
					weight = -0.5
				}
				interception = {
					reference = 12 
					weight = -0.15
				}
				intel = {
					weight = 0.1
					reference = 100
				}
			}

			disaster = {
				base = 0.05
				anti_air = {
					reference = 6
					weight = 0.30
				}
				interception = {
					reference = 12 
					weight = 0.15
				}
			}

			critical = {
				base = 0.01
				air_agility = {
					reference = 30
					start_reference = 10
					weight = 0.10
				}
				strategic_bomber = {
					weight = 0.15
					reference = 150
					start_reference = 100
				}
			}
		}
		success_levels = {
			failure = {
				victim_effects = {
					set_temp_variable = {
						missle_strike_dmg = 0
					}
					custom_effect_tooltip = {
						localization_key = bridges_raid_losses_tt
						LOSSES = [?missle_strike_dmg]
					}
				}
				actor_effects = {
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 12 }
					
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = raid_planes_lostt }	# missiles actually expended (shown by the loc)
					custom_effect_tooltip = {
						localization_key = missiles_raid_losses_tt
					}

					hidden_effect = {
						raid_damage_units = {
							plane_loss = raid_planes_lostt
							ratio = no
						}
					}
				}
			}
			limited_success = {
				victim_effects = {
					set_temp_variable = {
						missle_strike_dmg = 20
					}
					subtract_from_variable = {
						global.dnipro_obj_current_hp^15 = missle_strike_dmg
					}
					custom_effect_tooltip = {
						localization_key = bridges_raid_losses_tt
						LOSSES = [?missle_strike_dmg]
					}
				}
				actor_effects = {
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lostt = 100 }
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 1 }

					var:target_state = {
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.aa_defense = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@anti_air_building }
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.radar_mult = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@radar_station }
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.rocket_mult = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@rocket_site }
					}

					add_to_temp_variable = { radar_mult = 0.5 }
					multiply_temp_variable = { aa_defense = radar_mult }
					multiply_temp_variable = { rocket_mult = radar_mult }

					if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = { check_variable = { rocket_mult = 0 } }
						}
						multiply_temp_variable = { aa_defense = rocket_mult }

						if = {
							limit = {
								check_variable = { aa_defense = 0 }
							}
							add_to_temp_variable = { aa_defense = rocket_mult }
						}
					}

					multiply_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = aa_defense integer = yes }

					clamp_temp_variable = {
						var = raid_planes_lost
						min = 0
					}

					round_temp_variable = raid_planes_lost

					if = { 
						limit = { 
							check_variable = { raid_planes_lost > 12 } 
						} 
						set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 10 } 
					}

					if = { 
						limit = { 
							check_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 0 } 
						} 
						set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 4 } 
					}
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = raid_planes_lostt }	# missiles actually expended (shown by the loc)
					custom_effect_tooltip = {
						localization_key = missiles_raid_losses_tt
					}

					hidden_effect = {
						raid_damage_units = {
							plane_loss = raid_planes_lostt
							ratio = no
						}
					}
				}
				
			}
			success = {
				victim_effects = {
					# Random damage is applied hidden and stored; the result popup re-evaluates this block
					# after execution with a new roll, so the tooltip must read the stored value.
					hidden_effect = {
						set_temp_variable_to_random = {
							var = missle_strike_dmg
							min = 20
							max = 40
							integer = yes
						}
						subtract_from_variable = {
							global.dnipro_obj_current_hp^15 = missle_strike_dmg
						}
						set_variable = { global.raid_last_bridge_dmg = missle_strike_dmg }
					}
					set_temp_variable = { missle_strike_dmg = global.raid_last_bridge_dmg }	# stored real damage (shown by the loc)
					custom_effect_tooltip = {
						localization_key = bridges_raid_losses_tt
					}
				}
				actor_effects = {
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lostt = 100 }
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 1 }

					var:target_state = {
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.aa_defense = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@anti_air_building }
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.radar_mult = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@radar_station }
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.rocket_mult = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@rocket_site }
					}

					add_to_temp_variable = { radar_mult = 0.5 }
					multiply_temp_variable = { aa_defense = radar_mult }
					multiply_temp_variable = { rocket_mult = radar_mult }

					if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = { check_variable = { rocket_mult = 0 } }
						}
						multiply_temp_variable = { aa_defense = rocket_mult }

						if = {
							limit = {
								check_variable = { aa_defense = 0 }
							}
							add_to_temp_variable = { aa_defense = rocket_mult }
						}
					}

					multiply_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = aa_defense integer = yes }

					clamp_temp_variable = {
						var = raid_planes_lost
						min = 0
					}

					round_temp_variable = raid_planes_lost

					if = { 
						limit = { 
							check_variable = { raid_planes_lost > 10 } 
						} 
						set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 8 } 
					}

					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = raid_planes_lostt }	# missiles actually expended (shown by the loc)
					custom_effect_tooltip = {
						localization_key = missiles_raid_losses_tt
					}

					hidden_effect = {
						raid_damage_units = {
							plane_loss = raid_planes_lostt
							ratio = no
						}
					}
				}
				
			}
			critical_success = {
				victim_effects = {
					set_temp_variable = {
						missle_strike_dmg = 50
					}
					subtract_from_variable = {
						global.dnipro_obj_current_hp^15 = missle_strike_dmg
					}
					custom_effect_tooltip = {

						localization_key = bridges_raid_losses_tt
						LOSSES = [?missle_strike_dmg]
					}
				}
				actor_effects = {
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 12 }

						LOSSES = [?raid_planes_lost]

			limited_success = {
				victim_effects = {
					set_temp_variable = {
						missle_strike_dmg = 40
					}
					subtract_from_variable = {
						global.dnipro_obj_current_hp^16 = missle_strike_dmg
					}
					custom_effect_tooltip = {

						LOSSES = [?missle_strike_dmg]
					}
				}
				actor_effects = {
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lostt = 12 }
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 1 }

					var:target_state = {
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.aa_defense = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@anti_air_building }
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.radar_mult = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@radar_station }
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.rocket_mult = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@rocket_site }
					}

					add_to_temp_variable = { radar_mult = 0.5 }
					multiply_temp_variable = { aa_defense = radar_mult }
					multiply_temp_variable = { rocket_mult = radar_mult }

					if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = { check_variable = { rocket_mult = 0 } }
						}
						multiply_temp_variable = { aa_defense = rocket_mult }

						if = {
							limit = {
								check_variable = { aa_defense = 0 }
							}
							add_to_temp_variable = { aa_defense = rocket_mult }
						}
					}

					multiply_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = aa_defense integer = yes }

					clamp_temp_variable = {
						var = raid_planes_lost
						min = 0
					}

					round_temp_variable = raid_planes_lost

					if = { 
						limit = { 
							check_variable = { raid_planes_lost > 3 } 
						} 
						set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 3 } 
					}

					if = { 
						limit = { 
							check_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 0 } 
						} 
						set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 2 } 
					}
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = raid_planes_lostt }	# missiles actually expended (shown by the loc)
					custom_effect_tooltip = {
						localization_key = missiles_raid_losses_tt
					}

					hidden_effect = {
						raid_damage_units = {
							plane_loss = raid_planes_lostt
							ratio = no
						}
					}
				}
				
			}
			success = {
				victim_effects = {
					# Random damage is applied hidden and stored; the result popup re-evaluates this block
					# after execution with a new roll, so the tooltip must read the stored value.

						multiply_temp_variable = { aa_defense = rocket_mult }

						if = {
							limit = {
								check_variable = { aa_defense = 0 }
							}
							add_to_temp_variable = { aa_defense = rocket_mult }
						}
					}

					multiply_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = aa_defense integer = yes }

					clamp_temp_variable = {
						var = raid_planes_lost
						min = 0
					}

					round_temp_variable = raid_planes_lost

					if = { 
						limit = { 
							check_variable = { raid_planes_lost > 3 } 
						} 
						set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 3 } 
					}

					if = { 
						limit = { 
							check_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 0 } 
						} 
						set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 2 } 
					}
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = raid_planes_lostt }	# missiles actually expended (shown by the loc)
					custom_effect_tooltip = {
						localization_key = missiles_raid_losses_tt
					}

					hidden_effect = {
						raid_damage_units = {
							plane_loss = raid_planes_lostt
							ratio = no
						}
					}
				}
			}
			success = {
				victim_effects = {
					# Random damage is applied hidden and stored; the result popup re-evaluates this block
					# after execution with a new roll, so the tooltip must read the stored value.
					hidden_effect = {
						set_temp_variable_to_random = {
							var = missle_strike_dmg
							min = 20
							max = 40
							integer = yes
						}
						subtract_from_variable = {
							global.dnipro_obj_current_hp^17 = missle_strike_dmg
						}
						set_variable = { global.raid_last_bridge_dmg = missle_strike_dmg }
					}
					set_temp_variable = { missle_strike_dmg = global.raid_last_bridge_dmg }	# stored real damage (shown by the loc)
					custom_effect_tooltip = {
						localization_key = bridges_raid_losses_tt
					}
				}
				actor_effects = {
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lostt = 20 }
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 1 }

					var:target_state = {
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.aa_defense = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@anti_air_building }
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.radar_mult = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@radar_station }
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.rocket_mult = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@rocket_site }
					}

					add_to_temp_variable = { radar_mult = 0.5 }
					multiply_temp_variable = { aa_defense = radar_mult }
					multiply_temp_variable = { rocket_mult = radar_mult }

					if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = { check_variable = { rocket_mult = 0 } }
						}
						multiply_temp_variable = { aa_defense = rocket_mult }

						if = {
							limit = {
								check_variable = { aa_defense = 0 }
							}
							add_to_temp_variable = { aa_defense = rocket_mult }
						}
					}

					multiply_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = aa_defense integer = yes }

					clamp_temp_variable = {
						var = raid_planes_lost
						min = 0
					}

					round_temp_variable = raid_planes_lost

					if = { 
						limit = { 
							check_variable = { raid_planes_lost > 3 } 
						} 
						set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 3 } 
					}

					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = raid_planes_lostt }	# missiles actually expended (shown by the loc)
					custom_effect_tooltip = {
						localization_key = missiles_raid_losses_tt
					}

					hidden_effect = {
						raid_damage_units = {
							plane_loss = raid_planes_lostt
							ratio = no
						}
					}
				}
			}
			critical_success = {
				victim_effects = {
					set_temp_variable = {
						missle_strike_dmg = 50
					}
					subtract_from_variable = {
						global.dnipro_obj_current_hp^17 = missle_strike_dmg
					}
					custom_effect_tooltip = {
						localization_key = bridges_raid_losses_tt
						LOSSES = [?missle_strike_dmg]
					}
				}
				actor_effects = {
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lostt = 20 }
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 0 }

					var:target_state = {
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.aa_defense = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@anti_air_building }
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.radar_mult = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@radar_station }
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.rocket_mult = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@rocket_site }
					}

					add_to_temp_variable = { radar_mult = 0.5 }
					multiply_temp_variable = { aa_defense = radar_mult }
					multiply_temp_variable = { rocket_mult = radar_mult }

					if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = { check_variable = { rocket_mult = 0 } }
						}
						multiply_temp_variable = { aa_defense = rocket_mult }

						if = {
							limit = {
								check_variable = { aa_defense = 0 }
							}
							add_to_temp_variable = { aa_defense = rocket_mult }
						}
					}

					multiply_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = aa_defense integer = yes }

					clamp_temp_variable = {
						var = raid_planes_lost
						min = 0
					}

					round_temp_variable = raid_planes_lost

					if = { 
						limit = { 
							check_variable = { raid_planes_lost > 2 } 
						} 
						set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 2 }

					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = raid_planes_lostt }	# missiles actually expended (shown by the loc)

						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.aa_defense = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@anti_air_building }
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.radar_mult = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@radar_station }
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.rocket_mult = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@rocket_site }
					}

					add_to_temp_variable = { radar_mult = 0.5 }
					multiply_temp_variable = { aa_defense = radar_mult }
					multiply_temp_variable = { rocket_mult = radar_mult }

					if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = { check_variable = { rocket_mult = 0 } }
						}
						multiply_temp_variable = { aa_defense = rocket_mult }

						if = {
							limit = {
								check_variable = { aa_defense = 0 }
							}
							add_to_temp_variable = { aa_defense = rocket_mult }
						}
					}

					multiply_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = aa_defense integer = yes }

					clamp_temp_variable = {
						var = raid_planes_lost
						min = 0
					}

					round_temp_variable = raid_planes_lost

					if = { 
						limit = { 
							check_variable = { raid_planes_lost > 10 } 
						} 
						set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 8 } 
					}

					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = raid_planes_lostt }	# missiles actually expended (shown by the loc)
					custom_effect_tooltip = {
						localization_key = missiles_raid_losses_tt
					}

					hidden_effect = {
						raid_damage_units = {
							plane_loss = raid_planes_lostt
							ratio = no
						}
					}
				}
				
			}
			critical_success = {
				victim_effects = {
					set_temp_variable = {
						missle_strike_dmg = 50
					}
					subtract_from_variable = {
						global.dnipro_obj_current_hp^17 = missle_strike_dmg
					}
					custom_effect_tooltip = {
						localization_key = bridges_raid_losses_tt
						LOSSES = [?missle_strike_dmg]
					}
				}
				actor_effects = {
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lostt = 100 }
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 1 }

					var:target_state = {
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.aa_defense = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@anti_air_building }
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.radar_mult = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@radar_station }
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.rocket_mult = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@rocket_site }
					}

					add_to_temp_variable = { radar_mult = 0.5 }
					multiply_temp_variable = { aa_defense = radar_mult }
					multiply_temp_variable = { rocket_mult = radar_mult }

					if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = { check_variable = { rocket_mult = 0 } }
						}
						multiply_temp_variable = { aa_defense = rocket_mult }

						if = {
							limit = {
								check_variable = { aa_defense = 0 }
							}
							add_to_temp_variable = { aa_defense = rocket_mult }
						}
					}

					multiply_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = aa_defense integer = yes }

					clamp_temp_variable = {
						var = raid_planes_lost
						min = 0
					}

					round_temp_variable = raid_planes_lost

					if = { 
						limit = { 
							check_variable = { raid_planes_lost > 8 } 
						} 
						set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 7 } 
					}

					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = raid_planes_lostt }	# missiles actually expended (shown by the loc)
					custom_effect_tooltip = {
						localization_key = missiles_raid_losses_tt
					}

					hidden_effect = {
						raid_damage_units = {
							plane_loss = raid_planes_lostt
							ratio = no
						}
					}
				}
				
			}
		}

				amount = { min = 20 }
			}
		}

					reference = 12 
					weight = -0.15
				}

					reference = 100
				}
			}

				base = 0.05
				anti_air = {
					reference = 6
					weight = 0.30
				}
				interception = {
					reference = 12 
					weight = 0.15
				}
				air_agility = {
					reference = 200
					weight = -0.30

				base = 0.01
				air_agility = {

					weight = 0.05

					reference = 3000
					start_reference = 1100
				}
			}
		}
		success_levels = {
			failure = {

						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.radar_mult = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@radar_station }

						localization_key = bridges_raid_losses_tt
						LOSSES = [?missle_strike_dmg]
					}
				}
				actor_effects = {
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lostt = 12 }
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 0 }

					var:target_state = {
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.aa_defense = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@anti_air_building }
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.radar_mult = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@radar_station }
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.rocket_mult = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@rocket_site }
					}

					add_to_temp_variable = { radar_mult = 0.5 }
					multiply_temp_variable = { aa_defense = radar_mult }
					multiply_temp_variable = { rocket_mult = radar_mult }

					if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = { check_variable = { rocket_mult = 0 } }
						}
						multiply_temp_variable = { aa_defense = rocket_mult }

						if = {
							limit = {
								check_variable = { aa_defense = 0 }
							}
							add_to_temp_variable = { aa_defense = rocket_mult }
						}
					}

					multiply_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = aa_defense integer = yes }

					clamp_temp_variable = {
						var = raid_planes_lost
						min = 0
					}

					round_temp_variable = raid_planes_lost

					if = { 
						limit = { 
							check_variable = { raid_planes_lost > 2 } 
						} 
						set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 2 } 
					}

					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = raid_planes_lostt }	# missiles actually expended (shown by the loc)
					custom_effect_tooltip = {
						localization_key = missiles_raid_losses_tt
					}

					hidden_effect = {
						raid_damage_units = {
							plane_loss = raid_planes_lostt
							ratio = no
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}

						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.radar_mult = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@radar_station }

			}
		}
		success_factors = {
			success = {
				base = 0.1
				strategic_bomber = {
					weight = 1
					reference = 1000
					start_weight = -0.4
				}
				air_agility = {
					reference = 200
					weight = 0.4	
					start_weight = -0.4
				}
				anti_air = {
					reference = 6
					weight = -0.5
				}
				intel = {
					weight = 0.1
					reference = 100
				}
			}

			disaster = {
				base = 0.05
				anti_air = {
					reference = 6
					weight = 0.30
				}
				air_agility = {
					reference = 200
					weight = -0.30
				}
			}

			critical = {
				base = 0.01
				air_agility = {
					reference = 200
					start_reference = 100
					weight = 0.05
				}
				strategic_bomber = {
					weight = 0.50
					reference = 3000
					start_reference = 1100
				}
			}
		}
		success_levels = {
			failure = {
				victim_effects = {
					set_temp_variable = {
						missle_strike_dmg = 0
					}
					custom_effect_tooltip = {
						localization_key = bridges_raid_losses_tt
						LOSSES = [?missle_strike_dmg]
					}
				}
				actor_effects = {
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 12 }
					
					custom_effect_tooltip = {
						localization_key = plane_raid_losses_tt
						LOSSES = [?raid_planes_lost]
					}

					hidden_effect = {
						raid_damage_units = {
							plane_loss = raid_planes_lost
							ratio = no
						}
					}
				}
			}
			limited_success = {
				victim_effects = {
					set_temp_variable = {
						missle_strike_dmg = 40
					}
					subtract_from_variable = {
						global.dnipro_obj_current_hp^20 = missle_strike_dmg
					}
					custom_effect_tooltip = {
						localization_key = bridges_raid_losses_tt
						LOSSES = [?missle_strike_dmg]
					}
				}
				actor_effects = {
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lostt = 12 }
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 1 }

					var:target_state = {
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.aa_defense = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@anti_air_building }
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.radar_mult = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@radar_station }
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.rocket_mult = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@rocket_site }
					}

					add_to_temp_variable = { radar_mult = 0.5 }
					multiply_temp_variable = { aa_defense = radar_mult }
					multiply_temp_variable = { rocket_mult = radar_mult }

					if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = { check_variable = { rocket_mult = 0 } }
						}
						multiply_temp_variable = { aa_defense = rocket_mult }

						if = {
							limit = {
								check_variable = { aa_defense = 0 }
							}
							add_to_temp_variable = { aa_defense = rocket_mult }
						}
					}

					multiply_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = aa_defense integer = yes }

					clamp_temp_variable = {
						var = raid_planes_lost
						min = 0
					}

					round_temp_variable = raid_planes_lost

					if = { 
						limit = { 
							check_variable = { raid_planes_lost > 3 } 
						} 
						set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 3 } 
					}

					if = { 
						limit = { 
							check_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 0 } 
						} 
						set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 2 } 
					}
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = raid_planes_lostt }	# missiles actually expended (shown by the loc)
					custom_effect_tooltip = {
						localization_key = missiles_raid_losses_tt
					}

					hidden_effect = {
						raid_damage_units = {
							plane_loss = raid_planes_lostt
							ratio = no
						}
					}
				}
				
			}
			success = {
				victim_effects = {
					# Random damage is applied hidden and stored; the result popup re-evaluates this block
					# after execution with a new roll, so the tooltip must read the stored value.

							var = missle_strike_dmg
							min = 40
							max = 80
							integer = yes
						}
						subtract_from_variable = {
							global.dnipro_obj_current_hp^20 = missle_strike_dmg
						}
						set_variable = { global.raid_last_bridge_dmg = missle_strike_dmg }
					}
					set_temp_variable = { missle_strike_dmg = global.raid_last_bridge_dmg }	# stored real damage (shown by the loc)

						}
					}

				air_agility = {
					reference = 200
					weight = 0.4	
					start_weight = -0.4
				}
				anti_air = {
					reference = 6
					weight = -0.5
				}
				interception = {
					reference = 12 
					weight = -0.15
				}
				intel = {
					weight = 0.1
					reference = 100
				}
			}

			disaster = {
				base = 0.05
				anti_air = {
					reference = 6
					weight = 0.30
				}
				interception = {
					reference = 12 
					weight = 0.15
				}
				air_agility = {
					reference = 200
					weight = -0.30
				}
			}

			critical = {
				base = 0.01
				air_agility = {
					reference = 200
					start_reference = 100
					weight = 0.05
				}
				strategic_bomber = {
					weight = 0.50
					reference = 3000
					start_reference = 1100
				}
			}
		}
		success_levels = {
			failure = {
				victim_effects = {
					set_temp_variable = {
						missle_strike_dmg = 0
					}
					custom_effect_tooltip = {
						localization_key = bridges_raid_losses_tt
						LOSSES = [?missle_strike_dmg]
					}
				}
				actor_effects = {
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 20 }
					
					custom_effect_tooltip = {
						localization_key = plane_raid_losses_tt
						LOSSES = [?raid_planes_lost]
					}

					hidden_effect = {
						raid_damage_units = {
							plane_loss = raid_planes_lost
							ratio = no
						}
					}
				}
			}
			limited_success = {
				victim_effects = {
					set_temp_variable = {
						missle_strike_dmg = 20
					}
					subtract_from_variable = {
						global.dnipro_obj_current_hp^21 = missle_strike_dmg
					}
					custom_effect_tooltip = {
						localization_key = bridges_raid_losses_tt
						LOSSES = [?missle_strike_dmg]
					}
				}
				actor_effects = {
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lostt = 20 }
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 1 }

					var:target_state = {
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.aa_defense = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@anti_air_building }
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.radar_mult = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@radar_station }
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.rocket_mult = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@rocket_site }
					}

					add_to_temp_variable = { radar_mult = 0.5 }
					multiply_temp_variable = { aa_defense = radar_mult }
					multiply_temp_variable = { rocket_mult = radar_mult }

					if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = { check_variable = { rocket_mult = 0 } }
						}
						multiply_temp_variable = { aa_defense = rocket_mult }

						if = {
							limit = {
								check_variable = { aa_defense = 0 }
							}
							add_to_temp_variable = { aa_defense = rocket_mult }
						}
					}

					multiply_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = aa_defense integer = yes }

					clamp_temp_variable = {
						var = raid_planes_lost
						min = 0
					}

					round_temp_variable = raid_planes_lost

					if = { 
						limit = { 
							check_variable = { raid_planes_lost > 3 } 
						} 
						set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 3 } 
					}

					if = { 
						limit = { 
							check_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 0 } 
						} 
						set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 2 } 
					}
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = raid_planes_lostt }	# missiles actually expended (shown by the loc)
					custom_effect_tooltip = {
						localization_key = missiles_raid_losses_tt
					}

					hidden_effect = {
						raid_damage_units = {
							plane_loss = raid_planes_lostt
							ratio = no
						}
					}
				}
			}
			success = {
				victim_effects = {
					# Random damage is applied hidden and stored; the result popup re-evaluates this block
					# after execution with a new roll, so the tooltip must read the stored value.
					hidden_effect = {
						set_temp_variable_to_random = {
							var = missle_strike_dmg
							min = 20
							max = 40
							integer = yes
						}
						subtract_from_variable = {
							global.dnipro_obj_current_hp^21 = missle_strike_dmg
						}
						set_variable = { global.raid_last_bridge_dmg = missle_strike_dmg }
					}
					set_temp_variable = { missle_strike_dmg = global.raid_last_bridge_dmg }	# stored real damage (shown by the loc)
					custom_effect_tooltip = {
						localization_key = bridges_raid_losses_tt
					}
				}
				actor_effects = {
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lostt = 20 }
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 1 }

					var:target_state = {
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.aa_defense = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@anti_air_building }

					}

					add_to_temp_variable = { radar_mult = 0.5 }
					multiply_temp_variable = { aa_defense = radar_mult }
					multiply_temp_variable = { rocket_mult = radar_mult }

					if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = { check_variable = { rocket_mult = 0 } }
						}
						multiply_temp_variable = { aa_defense = rocket_mult }

						if = {
							limit = {
								check_variable = { aa_defense = 0 }
							}
							add_to_temp_variable = { aa_defense = rocket_mult }
						}
					}

					multiply_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = aa_defense integer = yes }

					clamp_temp_variable = {
						var = raid_planes_lost
						min = 0
					}

					round_temp_variable = raid_planes_lost

					if = { 
						limit = { 
							check_variable = { raid_planes_lost > 3 } 
						} 
						set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 3 } 
					}

					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = raid_planes_lostt }	# missiles actually expended (shown by the loc)
					custom_effect_tooltip = {

					}

					hidden_effect = {
						raid_damage_units = {
							plane_loss = raid_planes_lostt
							ratio = no
						}
					}
				}
			}
			critical_success = {
				victim_effects = {
					set_temp_variable = {
						missle_strike_dmg = 50
					}
					subtract_from_variable = {
						global.dnipro_obj_current_hp^21 = missle_strike_dmg
					}
					custom_effect_tooltip = {
						localization_key = bridges_raid_losses_tt
						LOSSES = [?missle_strike_dmg]
					}
				}
				actor_effects = {

					var:target_state = {

				}
				actor_effects = {
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lostt = 100 }
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 1 }

					var:target_state = {
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.aa_defense = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@anti_air_building }
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.radar_mult = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@radar_station }
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.rocket_mult = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@rocket_site }
					}

					add_to_temp_variable = { radar_mult = 0.5 }
					multiply_temp_variable = { aa_defense = radar_mult }
					multiply_temp_variable = { rocket_mult = radar_mult }

					if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = { check_variable = { rocket_mult = 0 } }
						}
						multiply_temp_variable = { aa_defense = rocket_mult }

						if = {
							limit = {
								check_variable = { aa_defense = 0 }
							}
							add_to_temp_variable = { aa_defense = rocket_mult }
						}
					}

					multiply_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = aa_defense integer = yes }

					clamp_temp_variable = {
						var = raid_planes_lost
						min = 0
					}

					round_temp_variable = raid_planes_lost

					if = { 
						limit = { 
							check_variable = { raid_planes_lost > 12 } 
						} 
						set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 10 } 
					}

					if = { 
						limit = { 
							check_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 0 } 
						} 
						set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 4 } 
					}
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = raid_planes_lostt }	# missiles actually expended (shown by the loc)
					custom_effect_tooltip = {
						localization_key = missiles_raid_losses_tt
					}

					hidden_effect = {
						raid_damage_units = {
							plane_loss = raid_planes_lostt
							ratio = no
						}
					}
				}
				
			}
			success = {
				victim_effects = {
					# Random damage is applied hidden and stored; the result popup re-evaluates this block
					# after execution with a new roll, so the tooltip must read the stored value.
					hidden_effect = {
						set_temp_variable_to_random = {
							var = missle_strike_dmg
							min = 20
							max = 40
							integer = yes
						}
						subtract_from_variable = {
							global.dnipro_obj_current_hp^21 = missle_strike_dmg
						}
						set_variable = { global.raid_last_bridge_dmg = missle_strike_dmg }
					}
					set_temp_variable = { missle_strike_dmg = global.raid_last_bridge_dmg }	# stored real damage (shown by the loc)
					custom_effect_tooltip = {
						localization_key = bridges_raid_losses_tt
					}
				}
				actor_effects = {
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lostt = 100 }
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 1 }

					var:target_state = {
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.aa_defense = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@anti_air_building }
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.radar_mult = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@radar_station }
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.rocket_mult = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@rocket_site }
					}

					add_to_temp_variable = { radar_mult = 0.5 }
					multiply_temp_variable = { aa_defense = radar_mult }
					multiply_temp_variable = { rocket_mult = radar_mult }

					if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = { check_variable = { rocket_mult = 0 } }
						}
						multiply_temp_variable = { aa_defense = rocket_mult }

						if = {
							limit = {
								check_variable = { aa_defense = 0 }
							}
							add_to_temp_variable = { aa_defense = rocket_mult }
						}
					}

					multiply_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = aa_defense integer = yes }

					clamp_temp_variable = {
						var = raid_planes_lost
						min = 0
					}

					round_temp_variable = raid_planes_lost

					if = { 
						limit = { 
							check_variable = { raid_planes_lost > 10 } 
						} 
						set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 8 } 
					}

					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = raid_planes_lostt }	# missiles actually expended (shown by the loc)
					custom_effect_tooltip = {
						localization_key = missiles_raid_losses_tt
					}

					hidden_effect = {
						raid_damage_units = {
							plane_loss = raid_planes_lostt
							ratio = no
						}
					}
				}
				
			}
			critical_success = {
				victim_effects = {
					set_temp_variable = {
						missle_strike_dmg = 50
					}
					subtract_from_variable = {
						global.dnipro_obj_current_hp^21 = missle_strike_dmg
					}
					custom_effect_tooltip = {
						localization_key = bridges_raid_losses_tt
						LOSSES = [?missle_strike_dmg]
					}
				}
				actor_effects = {
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lostt = 100 }
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 1 }

					var:target_state = {
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.aa_defense = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@anti_air_building }
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.radar_mult = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@radar_station }
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.rocket_mult = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@rocket_site }
					}

					add_to_temp_variable = { radar_mult = 0.5 }
					multiply_temp_variable = { aa_defense = radar_mult }
					multiply_temp_variable = { rocket_mult = radar_mult }

					if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = { check_variable = { rocket_mult = 0 } }
						}
						multiply_temp_variable = { aa_defense = rocket_mult }

						if = {
							limit = {
								check_variable = { aa_defense = 0 }
							}
							add_to_temp_variable = { aa_defense = rocket_mult }
						}
					}

					multiply_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = aa_defense integer = yes }

					clamp_temp_variable = {
						var = raid_planes_lost
						min = 0
					}

					round_temp_variable = raid_planes_lost

					if = { 
						limit = { 
							check_variable = { raid_planes_lost > 8 } 
						} 
						set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 7 } 
					}

					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = raid_planes_lostt }	# missiles actually expended (shown by the loc)
					custom_effect_tooltip = {

					# Random damage is applied hidden and stored; the result popup re-evaluates this block
					# after execution with a new roll, so the tooltip must read the stored value.
					hidden_effect = {
						set_temp_variable_to_random = {
							var = missle_strike_dmg
							min = 40
							max = 80
							integer = yes
						}
						subtract_from_variable = {
							global.dnipro_obj_current_hp^23 = missle_strike_dmg
						}
						set_variable = { global.raid_last_bridge_dmg = missle_strike_dmg }
					}
					set_temp_variable = { missle_strike_dmg = global.raid_last_bridge_dmg }	# stored real damage (shown by the loc)
					custom_effect_tooltip = {
						localization_key = bridges_raid_losses_tt
					}
				}
				actor_effects = {
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lostt = 12 }
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 1 }

					var:target_state = {
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.aa_defense = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@anti_air_building }
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.radar_mult = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@radar_station }
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.rocket_mult = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@rocket_site }
					}

					add_to_temp_variable = { radar_mult = 0.5 }
					multiply_temp_variable = { aa_defense = radar_mult }
					multiply_temp_variable = { rocket_mult = radar_mult }

					if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = { check_variable = { rocket_mult = 0 } }
						}
						multiply_temp_variable = { aa_defense = rocket_mult }

						if = {
							limit = {
								check_variable = { aa_defense = 0 }
							}
							add_to_temp_variable = { aa_defense = rocket_mult }
						}
					}

					multiply_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = aa_defense integer = yes }

					clamp_temp_variable = {
						var = raid_planes_lost
						min = 0
					}

					round_temp_variable = raid_planes_lost

					if = { 
						limit = { 
							check_variable = { raid_planes_lost > 3 } 
						} 
						set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 3 } 
					}

					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = raid_planes_lostt }	# missiles actually expended (shown by the loc)
					custom_effect_tooltip = {
						localization_key = missiles_raid_losses_tt
					}

					hidden_effect = {
						raid_damage_units = {
							plane_loss = raid_planes_lostt
							ratio = no
						}
					}
				}
				
			}
			critical_success = {
				victim_effects = {
					set_temp_variable = {
						missle_strike_dmg = 100
					}
					subtract_from_variable = {
						global.dnipro_obj_current_hp^23 = missle_strike_dmg
					}
					custom_effect_tooltip = {
						localization_key = bridges_raid_losses_tt
						LOSSES = [?missle_strike_dmg]
					}
				}
				actor_effects = {
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lostt = 12 }
					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 0 }

					var:target_state = {
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.aa_defense = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@anti_air_building }
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.radar_mult = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@radar_station }
						set_temp_variable = { ROOT.rocket_mult = THIS.non_damaged_building_level@rocket_site }
					}

					add_to_temp_variable = { radar_mult = 0.5 }
					multiply_temp_variable = { aa_defense = radar_mult }
					multiply_temp_variable = { rocket_mult = radar_mult }

					if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = { check_variable = { rocket_mult = 0 } }
						}
						multiply_temp_variable = { aa_defense = rocket_mult }

						if = {
							limit = {
								check_variable = { aa_defense = 0 }
							}
							add_to_temp_variable = { aa_defense = rocket_mult }
						}
					}

					multiply_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = aa_defense integer = yes }

					clamp_temp_variable = {
						var = raid_planes_lost
						min = 0
					}

					round_temp_variable = raid_planes_lost

					if = { 
						limit = { 
							check_variable = { raid_planes_lost > 2 } 
						} 
						set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = 2 } 
					}

					set_temp_variable = { raid_planes_lost = raid_planes_lostt }	# missiles actually expended (shown by the loc)
					custom_effect_tooltip = {
						localization_key = missiles_raid_losses_tt
					}

					hidden_effect = {
						raid_damage_units = {
							plane_loss = raid_planes_lostt
							ratio = no
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
